What variables in the raw data is `output` *Absolute position* derived from?\n\ni. Derived from the `position` behavior time series.\n\nii.\n```python\nposition = behavior_data['position'].data[:][:T]\nassert np.allclose(behavior_data['position'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for position do not match trial number timestamps\"\n...\nposition_curr = position[idx]\n```\n\niii. The `position` variable directly records the animal's position in the VR corridor in cm.\n\n## 8-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Absolute position*?\n\ni. No processing beyond extracting the per-trial slice and discretizing. Discretized into 5 bins using `np.digitize` with bin edges `[-inf, 90, 180, 270, 360, inf]`, corresponding to 90 cm wide bins spanning the 450 cm track. Within the converted trials the position ranges from -0.8 to 451.8 cm, so the open first and last bins absorb the few samples that fall just outside the track (185 of 1,307,665). \n\nii.\n```python\nposition_curr = position[idx]\noutput_curr[1] = np.digitize(position_curr, position_bins) - 1\n```\n\n```python\nposition_bins = [-np.inf,90,180,270,360,np.inf]\n...\noutput_curr[1] = np.digitize(position_curr, position_bins) - 1\n```\n\n\niii. The raw position values are used directly. Each lap starts at 0 cm and the track is 450 cm long (Methods), so five 90 cm bins over 0 to 450 are equal-sized by construction. The survey's wider -50 to 450 range comes from including the inter-trial teleport period, which is not part of any converted trial.\n\n## 8-c. How is `output` *Absolute position* thresholded into categories?\n\ni. Discretized into 5 bins using `np.digitize` with bin edges `[-inf, 90, 180, 270, 360, inf]`, corresponding to 90 cm wide bins spanning the 450 cm track. \n\nii.\n```python\nposition_bins = [-np.inf,90,180,270,360,np.inf]\n...\noutput_curr[1] = np.digitize(position_curr, position_bins) - 1\n```\n\niii. The instructions specify \"5 equal-sized bins\" spanning the 450 cm track, which is 90 cm per bin. The first and last edges are left open so that samples marginally outside the track fall in the end bins rather than in a class of their own.\n\n## 8-d. What variables in the raw data is `output` *Reward outcome* derived from?\n\ni. Derived from the `Reward` behavior time series. Reward events have their own timestamps.\n\nii.\n```python\nreward_times = behavior_data['Reward'].timestamps[:]\nreward_time_indices = np.searchsorted(timestamps, reward_times)\nisreward = np.zeros(T)\nisreward[reward_time_indices] = 1\n...\noutput_curr[5] = np.any(isreward[idx]).astype(int)\n```\n\niii. The `Reward` time series records reward delivery events with separate timestamps from the behavior sampling rate.\n\n## 11-b. What processing is involved in computing `output` *Reward outcome*?\n\ni. Reward event timestamps are mapped to the nearest behavior timepoint using `searchsorted`. For each trial, the output is 1 if any reward event occurred within the trial, 0 otherwise. The value is constant across all timepoints in the trial.\n\nii.\n```python\nreward_time_indices = np.searchsorted(timestamps, reward_times)\nerr = timestamps[reward_time_indices] - reward_times\nassert np.all(np.abs(err) <= time_bin_size/2)\nisreward = np.zeros(T)\nisreward[reward_time_indices] = 1\n...\noutput_curr[5] = np.any(isreward[idx]).astype(int)\n```\n\niii. The alignment error is checked to be within half a time bin. `np.any` is used because a trial either was rewarded or not — the specific timepoint doesn't matter for the per-trial binary output.\n\n## 12. How are minor mistakes in the data, e.g. missing data, handled?\n\ni. Several cases are handled:\n- **Neural/behavior length mismatch**: If the number of neural time bins differs from behavior timestamps, data is cropped to the minimum of the two with a warning printed.\n- **Short trials**: Trials with fewer than 50 timepoints (`min_ntimepoints`) are skipped with a warning.\n- **Missing reward zone data**: Trials where `reward_zone` is never active (all zeros) get NaN positions, and the Viterbi algorithm handles these via uniform emission (assignment driven by neighboring trials' transitions).\n- **Reward timestamp alignment**: An assertion verifies reward times align within half a time bin of behavior timestamps.\n\nii.\n```python\nif T != len(timestamps):\n    print(f\"Warning: number of neural time bins ({T}) does not match number of behavior timestamps ({len(timestamps)}), cropping to the minimum of the two\")\n    T = min(T, len(timestamps))\n    spike_data = spike_data[:T]\n    timestamps = timestamps[:T]\n...\nif idx.sum() < min_ntimepoints:\n    print(f\"Skipping trial {trial}...\")\n    istrial[trial] = False\n    continue\n```\n\niii. These are defensive checks found during data exploration. The neural/behavior mismatch occurs in a small number of sessions.\n\n## 13-a. What are the most time-consuming steps of the code?\n\ni. The most time-consuming steps are:\n1. **Loading NWB files** (`pynwb.NWBHDF5IO` and reading large arrays) — I/O bound\n2. **The survey step** — loads every NWB file to gather statistics and reward zone positions\n3. **Full conversion** — loads every NWB file again and extracts all trial data\n4. **Saving the .pkl file**\n\nii. N/A\n\niii. The NWB files are large (contain full neural recordings). Conversion is otherwise quite fast\n\n## 13-b. What loops in the code could have been vectorized to improve efficiency?\n\ni. The per-trial loop in `convert_session_data` (lines 267-332) iterates over each trial sequentially. Some operations like `np.digitize` and `compute_distance_to_reward_zone` could theoretically be applied to the full session arrays before splitting into trials, but the variable trial lengths make this awkward. The Viterbi inner loop (lines 503-507) iterates over states, which could be vectorized with matrix operations.\n\nii. N/A\n\niii. The per-trial loop is the natural structure given variable-length trials. Vectorizing would require padding or masking.\n\n## 13-c. What processing does the code repeat multiple times?\n\ni. The survey step and the conversion step both load every NWB file and read the same data arrays (neural, behavior). The survey reads neural data to count neurons and check rates, then the conversion reads it again. The `reward_zone_positions` are computed in the survey and then used in the conversion, but other data (position, speed, lick, etc.) is read twice. \n\nii. N/A\n\niii. I don't think these could be run at the same time, as it would require too much RAM. The Viterbi output created during surveying is required to process the data. It was convenient to run the survey before deciding how to handle the conversion of the data. \n\n## 13-d. What unnecessary processing does the code do that is discarded in downstream analyses?\n\ni. N/A\n\nii. N/A\n\niii. N/A\n\n## 13-e. How is memory usage optimized?\n\ni. N/A\n\nii. N/A\n\niii. N/A\n","numLines":591,"startLine":1,"totalLines":591}}}

Note that the threshold\n   281→        defaulted to 0.3 in the source function; the analyses always pass 0.5.\n   282→    :return: is_int (array) with a Boolean for each ROI, where putative interneuron is True.\n   283→\n   284→    ** copied from src/reward_relative/spatial.py::is_putative_interneuron **\n   285→    Change: takes the dF/F and speed arrays as arguments instead of reading them off a TwoPUtils \n   286→    `sess` object, which this conversion does not build.\n   287→    \"\"\"\n   288→    speed_corr = np.zeros((dff.shape[0],))\n   289→    nanmask = ~np.isnan(dff[0, :])\n   290→\n   291→    for c in range(dff.shape[0]):\n   292→        speed_corr[c] = np.corrcoef(\n   293→            dff[c, nanmask], speed[nanmask])[0, 1]\n   294→\n   295→    is_int = speed_corr > r_thresh\n   296→\n   297→    return is_int\n   298→\n   299→# end copy-paste\n   300→# =================================================================================================\n   301→\n   302→\n   303→def get_subjects(datadir=DATADIR):\n   304→    # reward_amounts are all the same\n   358→        reward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:]\n   359→        info['unique_reward_amounts'] = np.unique(reward_amounts)\n   360→        info['nrewards'] = len(reward_amounts)\n   361→        # autoreward is all 0s, so not useful for modeling, but check that it is all zeros\n   362→        autoreward = behavior_data['autoreward'].data[:]\n   363→        info['unique_autoreward'] = np.unique(autoreward[istrial])\n   364→        # vr environment\n   365→        vr_environment = behavior_data['environment'].data[:] # one value per time point, does this change by session?\n   366→        info['unique_vr_environment'] = np.unique(vr_environment[istrial])\n   367→        licks = behavior_data['lick'].data[:]\n   368→        info['unique_licks'] = np.unique(licks[istrial])\n   369→        position = behavior_data['position'].data[:]\n   370→        info['position_range'] = (position[istrial].min(), position[istrial].max())\n   371→        reward_zone = behavior_data['reward_zone'].data[:]\n   372→        info['unique_reward_zone'] = np.unique(reward_zone[istrial])\n   373→        scanning = behavior_data['scanning'].data[:]\n   374→        info['unique_scanning'] = np.unique(scanning[istrial])\n   375→        speed = behavior_data['speed'].data[:]\n   376→        info['speed_range'] = (speed[istrial].min(), speed[istrial].max())\n   377→        teleport = behavior_data['teleport'].data[:]\n   378→        info['unique_teleport'] = np.unique(teleport[istrial])\n   379→        \n   380→        # trial number and trial start do not agree with each other\n   381→        # use trial_start and teleport to find starts and ends of trials\n   382→        trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx = get_trial_starts_ends(behavior_data['trial_start'].data[:], teleport)\n   383→        trial_length = trial_end_idx - trial_start_idx\n   384→        info['trial_length_range'] = (trial_length.min(), trial_length.max())\n   385→\n   386→        ntrials = len(trial_start_idx)\n   387→        info['ntrials'] = ntrials\n   388→        info['reward_zone_positions'] = np.full((ntrials, 2), np.nan)\n   389→        for trial in range(ntrials):\n   390→            idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial], trial_end_idx[trial], dtype=int)\n   391→            pos_trial = position[idx]\n   392→            reward_zone_trial = reward_zone[idx] > 0\n   393→            if not np.any(reward_zone_trial):\n   394→                continue\n   395→            info['reward_zone_positions'][trial] = [np.min(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial]), np.max(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial])]\n   396→                    \n   397→    return info\n   398→    \n   399→def get_bin_centers(bin_edges,data):\n   400→    # replace infinity with the max/min of the data for plotting purposes\n   401→    bin_edges_plot = np.array(bin_edges.copy())\n   402→    if np.isinf(bin_edges_plot[0]):\n   403→        bin_edges_plot[0] = data.min()\n   404→        if bin_edges_plot[0] > bin_edges_plot[1]:\n   405→            bin_edges_plot[0] = 2*bin_edges_plot[1] - bin_edges_plot[2] # extrapolate to get a lower value for the first bin edge\n   406→    if np.isinf(bin_edges_plot[-1]):\n   407→        bin_edges_plot[-1] = data.max()\n   408→        if bin_edges_plot[-1] < bin_edges_plot[-2]:\n   409→            bin_edges_plot[-1] = 2*bin_edges_plot[-2] - bin_edges_plot[-3] # extrapolate to get a higher value for the last bin edge\n   410→    bin_centers = (bin_edges_plot[:-1] + bin_edges_plot[1:]) / 2\n   411→    return bin_centers\n   412→    \n   413→def compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position_curr, reward_zone_curr):\n   414→    ntimepoints = len(position_curr)\n   415→    reward_zone_range = reward_zone_dict[reward_zone_curr]\n   416→    distance_to_reward_zone = np.zeros(ntimepoints)\n   417→    idx_before_zone = position_curr < reward_zone_range[0]\n   418→    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_before_zone] = position_curr[idx_before_zone] - reward_zone_range[0] # negative if before the zone\n   419→    idx_after_zone = position_curr > reward_zone_range[1]\n   420→    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_after_zone] = position_curr[idx_after_zone] - reward_zone_range[1] # positive if after the zone\n   421→    return distance_to_reward_zone\n   422→    \n   423→def convert_session_data(subject, session, info, trial_offset, time_bin_size=None, datadir=DATADIR, \n   424→                         show_processing=False, max_neurons_plot=30, min_ntimepoints=50,\n   425→                         figfile=None):\n   426→\n   427→    nwb_file_path = get_nwb_file(subject, session, datadir)\n   428→    io = pynwb.NWBHDF5IO(nwb_file_path, 'r')\n   429→    nwbfile = io.read()\n   430→\n   431→    # parse neural activity data: the raw suite2p traces the paper computes its dF/F and\n   432→    # deconvolved events from. The deconvolution needs the trial boundaries, so it runs further\n   433→    # below, once those have been parsed out of the behavior data.\n   434→    fluorescence_data = nwbfile.processing[\"ophys\"][\"Fluorescence\"]\n   435→    neuropil_data = nwbfile.processing[\"ophys\"][\"Neuropil\"]\n   436→    fluorescence = []\n   437→    neuropil = []\n   438→    nplanes = len(fluorescence_data.roi_response_series)\n   439→    for plane_name, plane_data in fluorescence_data.roi_response_series.items():\n   440→        roi_data = plane_data.rois[:]\n   441→        iscell = np.array(roi_data['iscell'].to_list())[:,0] == 1\n   442→        fluorescence_curr = plane_data.data[:]\n   443→        neuropil_curr = neuropil_data.roi_response_series[plane_name].data[:]\n   444→        assert fluorescence_curr.shape[1] == iscell.shape[0], f\"Number of neurons in data ({fluorescence_curr.shape[1]}) does not match number of iscell entries ({iscell.shape[0]})\"\n   445→        assert neuropil_curr.shape == fluorescence_curr.shape, f\"Neuropil shape ({neuropil_curr.shape}) does not match fluorescence shape ({fluorescence_curr.shape})\"\n   446→        # Subset to the curated cells now rather than after dff. Every step of dff is per-cell --\n   447→        # its only cross-cell operation is a Gaussian with sigma 0 on the cell axis -- so this is\n   448→        # numerically identical to subsetting afterwards, on a third of the memory.\n   449→        fluorescence.append(fluorescence_curr[:,iscell])\n   450→        neuropil.append(neuropil_curr[:,iscell])\n   451→    T = fluorescence[0].shape[0]\n   452→    # The rate stored on the series is the scanner rate: with two planes interleaved it is twice\n   453→    # the rate at which any one plane is sampled, which is why dff takes n_planes as well.\n   454→    frame_rate = plane_data.rate\n   455→    neural_time_bin_size = nplanes/frame_rate*1000 # ms\n   456→\n   457→    behavior_data = nwbfile.processing['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series\n   458→    \n   459→    timestamps = behavior_data['trial number'].timestamps[:] \n   460→    if T != len(timestamps):\n   461→        print(f\"Warning: number of neural time bins ({T}) does not match number of behavior timestamps ({len(timestamps)}), cropping to the minimum of the two\")\n   462→        T = min(T, len(timestamps))\n   463→        fluorescence = [f[:T] for f in fluorescence]\n   464→        neuropil = [f[:T] for f in neuropil]\n   465→        timestamps = timestamps[:T]\n   466→    \n   467→    behavior_time_bin_size = np.median(np.diff(timestamps))*1000 # ms\n   468→    \n   469→    # trial number for each time point\n   470→    trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx = get_trial_starts_ends(behavior_data['trial_start'].data[:], behavior_data['teleport'].data[:])\n   471→    ntrials = len(trial_start_idx)\n   472→    \n   473→    # reward_amounts are all the same\n   474→    reward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:][:T]\n   475→    assert np.allclose(reward_amounts,reward_amounts[0]), f\"Reward amounts are not all the same, unique values: {np.unique(reward_amounts)}\"\n   476→    reward_times = behavior_data['Reward'].timestamps[:] # in seconds        \n   477→    # find the closest timestamp to reward times, not careful for mismatches right now\n   478→    reward_time_indices = np.searchsorted(timestamps, reward_times)\n   479→    err = timestamps[reward_time_indices] - reward_times\n   480→    assert np.all(np.abs(err) <= time_bin_size/2), f\"Reward times do not align with neural time bins, max error: {np.max(np.abs(err))} s\"\n   481→    isreward = np.zeros(T)\n   482→    isreward[reward_time_indices] = 1\n   483→\n   484→    # autoreward is all 0s, so not useful for modeling, but check that it is all zeros\n   485→\n   486→    # vr environment\n   487→    vr_environment = behavior_data['environment'].data[:][:T] # one value per time point, does this change by session?\n   488→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['environment'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for environment do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   489→\n   490→    # lick\n   491→    licks = behavior_data['lick'].data[:][:T]\n   492→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['lick'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for lick do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   493→\n   494→    # current position in the VR environment\n   495→    position = behavior_data['position'].data[:][:T]\n   496→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['position'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for position do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   497→\n   498→    # teleport -- not sure what to do with this, maybe should end the trial early??\n   499→    #isteleport = behavior_data['teleport'].data[:][:T] == 1\n   500→    #assert np.allclose(behavior_data['teleport'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for teleport do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   501→\n   502→    # reward zone -- take from pre-segmented data in info\n   503→    reward_zone = info['reward_zone_labels'][subject][trial_offset:trial_offset+ntrials]\n   504→\n   505→    # scanning is all 1s, so not useful for modeling\n   506→    \n   507→    # speed\n   508→    speed = behavior_data['speed'].data[:][:T] # cm / s\n   509→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['speed'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for speed do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   510→\n   511→    io.close()\n   512→\n   513→    assert len(timestamps) == T, f\"Number of timestamps ({len(timestamps)}) does not match number of neural time bins ({T})\"\n   514→    if time_bin_size is not None:\n   515→        assert np.isclose(neural_time_bin_size, time_bin_size, rtol=1e-2), f\"Neural time bin size ({neural_time_bin_size} ms) does not match specified time bin size ({time_bin_size} ms)\"\n   516→        assert np.isclose(behavior_time_bin_size, time_bin_size, rtol=1e-2), f\"Behavior time bin size ({behavior_time_bin_size} ms) does not match specified time bin size ({time_bin_size} ms)\"\n   517→    \n   518→    # neural activity: the paper's deconvolved events, computed per plane because the\n   519→    # deconvolution kernel depends on the per-plane sampling rate, frame_rate/nplanes.\n   520→    #\n   521→    # The released subject id m<N> is the paper's internal animal GCAMP<N>: methods.txt describes\n   522→    # exactly the two groups this table holds -- \"except for mice m11-m14 on task days 1, 7, 8 and\n   523→    # 14, and mice m15-m19 on day 1 and all switch days\" -- and names m17 and m18 as the two\n   524→    # animals imaged in two planes, which are the two-plane sessions in this dataset. Session\n   525→    # number is the experiment day: m11 starts at ses-03, and the paper says imaging for m11\n   526→    # started on day 3.\n   527→    keep_teleports = session in teleport_sessions.get(f'GCAMP{subject[1:]}', {}).get('exp_days', [])\n   528→    spike_data = []\n   529→    nint = 0\n   530→    for fluorescence_curr, neuropil_curr in zip(fluorescence, neuropil):\n   531→        # The trial indices are passed in shifted by +1: dff slices its windows as\n   532→        # [start-1, stop-1), one sample earlier than the [trial_start, teleport) laps emitted here\n   533→        # -- which is the window their own behavior.py:57 takes from the same two index arrays, so\n   534→        # the two files already disagree by a sample upstream. Unshifted, the last sample of every\n   535→        # lap comes out NaN (80 per session here) and one pre-lap sample gets a value instead;\n   536→        # shifted, dff's windows land exactly on the emitted laps. The one cost is that on\n   537→        # keep_teleports sessions the baseline window opens two samples after the teleport onset\n   538→        # rather than their one.\n   539→        dff_curr, events_curr = dff(fluorescence_curr.T.astype(np.float64),\n   540→                                    pd.Series(trial_start_idx + 1), pd.Series(trial_end_idx + 1),\n   541→                                    f_neu=neuropil_curr.T.astype(np.float64),\n   542→                                    neuropil_method='subtract', baseline_method='maximin',\n   543→                                    subtract_baseline=True, neu_coef=0.7, tau=0.7,\n   544→                                    frame_rate=frame_rate, n_planes=nplanes, deconvolve=True,\n   545→                                    keep_teleports=keep_teleports)\n   546→        # Drop the putative interneurons the paper excludes from further analysis.\n   547→        isint = is_putative_interneuron(dff_curr, speed)\n   548→        nint += int(np.sum(isint))\n   549→        spike_data.append(events_curr[~isint].T)\n   550→    spike_data = np.concatenate(spike_data, axis=1)\n   551→    print(f\"Excluded {nint} putative interneurons of {nint + spike_data.shape[1]} cells \"\n   552→          f\"({100*nint/(nint + spike_data.shape[1]):.2f}%)\")\n   553→    # Samples between laps are NaN by construction (that is what restricting the baseline to the\n   554→    # lap means); samples inside a lap must not be, since those are what the decoder sees.\n   555→    on_trial = np.zeros(T, dtype=bool)\n   556→    for start, stop in zip(trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx):\n   557→        on_trial[start:stop] = True\n   558→    assert not np.any(np.isnan(spike_data[on_trial])), \\\n   559→        \"Deconvolved events are undefined somewhere inside a trial\"\n   560→\n   561→    neural = []\n   562→    input = []\n   563→    output = []\n   564→    ninputs = len(input_names)\n   565→    noutputs = len(output_names)\n   566→    istrial = np.ones(ntrials,dtype=bool) # keep track of whether we have dropped a trial for having too few time points\n   567→    min_distance_to_reward_zone = np.inf\n   568→    max_distance_to_reward_zone = -np.inf\n   569→    for trial in range(ntrials):\n   570→        idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial], trial_end_idx[trial], dtype=int)\n   571→        if idx.sum() < min_ntimepoints:\n   572→            print(f\"Skipping trial {trial} in subject {subject} session {session} because it has only {idx.sum()} < {min_ntimepoints} time points\")\n   573→            istrial[trial] = False\n   574→            continue\n   575→\n   576→        spike_data_curr = spike_data[idx]\n   577→        timestamps_curr = timestamps[idx]\n   578→        vr_environment_curr = vr_environment[idx].astype(int)\n   579→        position_curr = position[idx]\n   580→        speed_curr = speed[idx]\n   581→        licks_curr = licks[idx]\n   582→\n   583→        ntimepoints = len(timestamps_curr)\n   584→        \n   585→        # neural data\n   586→        neural.append(spike_data_curr.T)\n   587→\n   588→        # time from trial start\n   589→        input_curr = np.full((ninputs, ntimepoints), np.nan)\n   590→        input_curr[0] = timestamps_curr - timestamps_curr[0]\n   591→        \n   592→        # environment\n   593→        input_curr[1] = vr_environment_curr\n   594→        \n   595→        # trial number\n   596→        input_curr[2] = trial\n   597→        \n   598→        # previous reward outcome\n   599→        if trial == 0:\n   600→            input_curr[3,:] = 0 # no previous trial, so set to 0\n   601→        else:\n   602→            prev_reward_idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial-1], trial_end_idx[trial-1], dtype=int)\n   603→            isprevreward = np.any(isreward[prev_reward_idx])\n   604→            input_curr[3,:] = int(isprevreward)\n   605→            \n   606→        input.append(input_curr)\n   607→        \n   608→        output_curr = np.full((noutputs, ntimepoints), -1)\n   609→\n   610→        # distance to reward zone\n   611→        reward_zone_curr = reward_zone[trial].item()\n   612→        distance_to_reward_zone = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position_curr, reward_zone_curr)\n   613→        min_distance_to_reward_zone = min(distance_to_reward_zone.min(), min_distance_to_reward_zone)\n   614→        max_distance_to_reward_zone = max(distance_to_reward_zone.max(), max_distance_to_reward_zone)\n   615→\n   616→        # histogram distance to reward zone into the specified bins\n   617→        output_curr[0] = np.digitize(distance_to_reward_zone, distance_to_reward_zone_bins) - 1 # 0 not possible\n   618→        \n   619→        # position\n   620→        output_curr[1] = np.digitize(position_curr, position_bins) - 1\n   621→        \n   622→        # speed\n   623→        output_curr[2] = np.digitize(speed_curr, speed_bins) - 1\n   624→        \n   625→        # lick\n   626→        output_curr[3] = (licks_curr > 0).astype(int)\n   627→        \n   628→        # reward location\n   629→        output_curr[4] = ['A','B','C'].index(reward_zone_curr)\n   630→        \n   631→        # reward outcome\n   632→        output_curr[5] = np.any(isreward[idx]).astype(int)\n   633→        \n   634→        output.append(output_curr)\n   635→        \n   636→    if show_processing:\n   637→        \n   638→        ntrials = len(neural)\n   639→        trials_plot = np.linspace(0, ntrials-1, min(ntrials, 5), dtype=int)\n   640→        nbehplot = 5\n   641→        if not np.isinf(max_neurons_plot):\n   642→            sharey = 'row'\n   643→        else:\n   644→            sharey = False\n   645→        fig, ax = plt.subplots(nbehplot+1, 5, figsize=(20, 20),\n   646→                                gridspec_kw={'height_ratios': [nbehplot,] + [1]*nbehplot},\n   647→                                sharex='col', sharey=sharey)\n   648→        \n   649→        distance_bin_centers = get_bin_centers(distance_to_reward_zone_bins, np.array([min_distance_to_reward_zone, max_distance_to_reward_zone]))\n   650→        position_bin_centers = get_bin_centers(position_bins, position[trial_start_idx[0]:trial_end_idx[-1]])\n   651→        speed_bin_centers = get_bin_centers(speed_bins, speed[trial_start_idx[0]:trial_end_idx[-1]])\n   652→        \n   653→        for triali, trial in enumerate(trials_plot):\n   654→            input_curr = input[trial]\n   655→            output_curr = output[trial]\n   656→            neural_curr = neural[trial]\n   657→            nneurons = np.minimum(max_neurons_plot,neural_curr.shape[0])\n   658→            idx = np.maximum(1,np.percentile(spike_data[spike_data[:,neuroni]>0,neuroni], 95))\n   662→                ax[0, triali].plot(timestamps_curr,  neural_curr[neuroni] / z + neuroni) # plot each neuron with an offset for visibility\n   663→\n   664→            ax[1,triali].plot(timestamps_curr,input_curr[1],'.-') # vr environment\n   665→            \n   666→            reward_zone_curr = reward_zone[trial].item()\n   667→            position_curr = position[idx]\n   668→            speed_curr = speed[idx]\n   669→            licks_curr = licks[idx]\n   670→            distance_to_reward_zone = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position_curr, reward_zone_curr)\n   671→            ax[2,triali].plot(timestamps_curr,distance_to_reward_zone,'.-',label='raw') # distance to reward zone\n   672→            ax[2,triali].plot(timestamps_curr,distance_bin_centers[output_curr[0]],'-',label='thresholded') # thresholded distance to reward zone\n   673→            ax[3,triali].plot(timestamps_curr,position_curr,'.-',label='raw') # position\n   674→            ax[3,triali].plot(timestamps_curr,position_bin_centers[output_curr[1]],'-',label='thresholded') # ax[4,triali].plot(timestamps_curr,speed_bin_centers[output_curr[2]],'-',label='thresholded') # thresholded speed\n   677→            ax[5,triali].plot(timestamps_curr,licks_curr,'.-',label='raw') #      ax[0,0].set_ylim(-1, max_neurons_plot+1)\n   682→        ax[0,0].set_ylabel('Neural activity')\n   683→        ax[1,0].set_ylabel('VR environment')\n   684→        ax[2,0].set_ylabel('Distance to reward zone (cm)')\n   685→        ax[2,0].legend()\n   686→        ax[3,0].set_ylabel('Position (cm)')\n   687→        ax[4,0].set_ylabel('Speed (cm/s)')\n   688→        ax[5,0].set_ylabel('Lick')\n   689→        \n   690→        plt.tight_layout()\n   691→        plt.show()\n   692→        if figfile is not None:\n   693→            fig.savefig(figfile)\n   694→        \n   695→    return neural, input, output\n   696→\n   697→def survey(force=False, datadir=DATADIR, surveyfile=SURVEYFILE):\n   698→    if not force and os.path.exists(surveyfile):\n   699→        print(f\"Loading existing survey from {surveyfile}\")\n   700→        with open(surveyfile, 'rb') as f:\n   701→            return pickle.load(f)\n   702→    all_info = {}\n   703→    subjects = get_subjects(datadir=datadir)\n   704→    all_info['reward_zone_positions'] = {}\n   705→    fig,ax = plt.subplots(1,len(subjects), figsize=(2*len(subjects),20),sharex=True,sharey=True)\n   706→\n   707→    for i,subject in enumerate(subjects):\n   708→        sessions = get_sessions(subject, datadir=datadir)\n   709→        print(f\"Subject: {subject}, Sessions: {sessions}\")\n   710→        all_info['reward_zone_positions'][subject] = np.zeros((0,2))\n   711→        for session in sessions:\n   712→            info = survey_session_data(subject, session, datadir=datadir)\n   713→            print(f\"Subject {subject}, Session {session}:\")\n   714→            for key, value in info.items():\n   715→                print(f\"  {key}: {value}\")\n   716→            for key,value in info.items():\n   717→                if key == 'reward_zone_positions':\n   718→                    all_info['reward_zone_positions'][subject] = np.concatenate((all_info['reward_zone_positions'][subject], value), axis=0)\n   719→                elif key == 'neural_time_bin_size':\n   720→                    if 'neural_time_bin_size' not in all_info:\n   721→                        all_info['neural_time_bin_size'] = [value,]\n   722→                    else:\n   723→                        all_info['neural_time_bin_size'].append(value)\n   724→                elif not ('range' in key or 'unique' in key):\n   725→                    if 'range_'+key not in all_info:\n   726→                        all_info['range_'+key] = (value, value)\n   727→                    else:\n   728→                        all_info['range_'+key] = (min(all_info.get('range_'+key, (value, value))[0], value), max(all_info.get('range_'+key, (value, value))[1], value))\n   729→                elif key not in all_info:\n   730→                    all_info[key] = value\n   731→                elif 'range' in key:\n   732→                    all_info[key] = (min(all_info[key][0], value[0]), max(all_info[key][1], value[1]))\n   733→                elif 'unique' in key:\n   734→                    all_info[key] = 771→\n   772→def segment_reward_positions(reward_zone_positions):\n   773→    # assign each trial to one of the reward zones defined in reward_zone_dict\n   774→    # use viterbi algorithm with a high cost for switching to encourage stable assignments\n   775→    zone_names = sorted(reward_zone_dict.keys())\n   776→    n_states = len(zone_names)\n   777→    zone_ranges = np.array([reward_zone_dict[z] for z in zone_names])  # (n_states, 2)\n   778→    pstay = .9\n   779→    sigma = 10.0  # std dev for Gaussian emission on zone distance\n   780→\n   781→    # transition log-probabilities\n   782→    log_trans = np.full((n_states, n_states), np.log((1 - pstay) / (n_states - 1)))\n   783→    np.fill_diagonal(log_trans, np.log(pstay))\n   784→\n   785→    results = {}\n   786→\n   787→    for mouse_id, positions in reward_zone_positions.items():\n   788→        n_trials = positions.shape[0]\n   789→        startpoints = positions[:,0] # use the start point as it looks cleaner\n   790→        valid = ~np.isnan(startpoints)\n   791→\n   792→        # emission log-probabilities: Gaussian penalty based on distance to zone range\n   793→        # distance is 0 if inside [lo, hi], otherwise distance to nearest edge\n   794→        # for NaN trials, uniform emission (all zeros) so assignment is driven by transitions\n   795→        log_emission = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n   796→        dist = np.maximum(zone_ranges[None, :, 0] - startpoints[valid, None],\n   797→                          startpoints[valid, None] - zone_ranges[None, :, 1]) # negative if inside the zone, positive if outside\n   798→        dist = np.maximum(dist, 0)\n   799→        log_emission[valid] = -0.5 * (dist / sigma) ** 2\n   800→\n   801→        # viterbi forward pass\n   802→        log_delta = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n   803→        psi = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states), dtype=int)\n   804→        log_delta[0] = log_emission[0]  # uniform prior\n   805→        for t in range(1, n_trials):\n   806→            for k in range(n_states):\n   807→                scores = log_delta[t - 1] + log_trans[:, k]\n   808→                psi[t, k] = np.argmax(scores)\n   809→                log_delta[t, k] = scores[psi[t, k]] + log_emission[t, k]\n   810→\n   811→        # backtrack\n   812→        states = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=int)\n   813→        states[-1] = np.argmax(log_delta[-1])\n   814→        for t in range(n_trials - 2, -1, -1):\n   815→            states[t] = psi[t + 1, states[t + 1]]\n   816→\n   817→        # convert state indices to zone names\n   818→        zone_labels = np.array([zone_names[s] for s in states])\n   819→\n   820→        # distance from each trial's startpoint to its assigned zone range\n   821→        assigned_lo = zone_ranges[states, 0]\n   822→        assigned_hi = zone_ranges[states, 1]\n   823→        zone_dist = np.maximum(np.maximum(assigned_lo - startpoints, startpoints - assigned_hi), 0)\n   824→        zone_dist[~valid] = np.nan\n   825→\n   826→        results[mouse_id] = {'zone_labels': zone_labels, 'states': states, 'zone_dist': zone_dist}\n   827→\n   828→    return results\n find nans\n    nan_inds = np.isnan(a)\n    a_nanless = np.copy(a)\n    # make nans 0\n    a_nanless[nan_inds] = 0\n\n    # inversely weight nanned indices\n    one = np.ones(a.shape)\n    one[nan_inds] = .001\n    a_nanless = ndimage.gaussian_filter(a_nanless, sig) # was filters.gaussian_filter\n    one = ndimage.gaussian_filter(one, sig) # was filters.gaussian_filter\n    return a_nanless / one\n\n\ndef nansmooth_1d(a, sig, axis=-1, return_nans=False, **kwargs):\n    \"\"\"\n    apply Gaussian smoothing to matrix A containing nans with kernel sig\n    without propagating nans\n    :param a:\n    :param sig:\n    :return:\n\n    ** copied from src/reward_relative/utilities.py::nansmooth **\n    (renamed: the TwoPUtils N-D version above already occupies the name `nansmooth`)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # find nans\n    nan_inds = np.isnan(a)\n    a_nanless = np.copy(a)\n    # make nans 0\n    a_nanless[nan_inds] = 0\n\n    # inversely weight nanned indices\n    one = np.ones(a.shape)\n    one[nan_inds] = 0.001\n    a_nanless = ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(a_nanless, sig, axis=axis, **kwargs) # was filters.gaussian_filter\n    one = ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(one, sig, axis=axis, **kwargs) # was filters.gaussian_filter\n\n    out = a_nanless / one\n    if return_nans:\n        out[nan_inds] = np.nan\n\n    return out\n\ndef dff(f,\n        trial_starts,\n        teleports,\n        f_neu=None,\n        neuropil_method=None,\n        baseline_method='maximin',\n        subtract_baseline=True,\n        neu_coef=0.7,\n        tau=0.7,\n        frame_rate=15,\n        n_planes=1,\n        deconvolve=False,\n        keep_teleports=False):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate dFF for 1 channel\n\n    :param f: ROI fluorescence timeseries\n    :param trial_starts:\n    :param teleports:\n    :param f_neu: neuropil fluorescence timeseries\n    :param baseline_method:\n    :param subtract_baseline: True gives ∆F/F, False just normalizes F to the basline (divides only)\n    :param neu_coef:\n    :param deconvolve: whether to deconvolve the dff trace into \"events\" (psuedo spikes)\n    :return: dff and spks if deconvolve is True, otherwise just dff\n\n    `trial_starts` and `teleports` must be pandas Series: the keep_teleports branch calls\n    `.to_list()` on `teleports`, which a numpy array does not have. That is their code, unchanged.\n\n    ** copied from src/reward_relative/preprocessing.py::dff **\n    Keeps only the branches this dataset can reach (single-channel)\n    Dropped: bleedthrough_ts, neu_bleedthrough_ts, regress_ts, scrub_ts, neuropil_method='regress',\n    baseline_method='maxsmooth', subtract_baseline=False. \n    \"\"\"\n\n    print('baseline method =', baseline_method)\n\n    # Check frame rate if deconvolving\n    if deconvolve:\n        if (frame_rate/n_planes) > 16:\n            warnings.warn(\n                f\"Using frame rate per plane: {(frame_rate/n_planes)} -- is this correct?\")\n\n    f_ = np.zeros(f.shape) * np.nan\n\n    if f_neu is not None:\n        f_neu_ = np.zeros(f_neu.shape) * np.nan\n\n    if keep_teleports:\n        # if keeping the teleports, keep all the fluorescence from the start of imaging\n        # EXCEPT the teleport sample itself, as this position values might be interpolated between\n        # the end of the track and -50 (where the teleport jitter starts)\n        print(\"keeping teleports\")\n        start_inds = np.append(\n            trial_starts[0],\n            np.array(teleports[:-1] + 2)\n        ).tolist()\n        # ^ add 2 so with start-1 we will start 1 sample after the teleport\n        stop_inds = teleports.to_list()\n        # we will subtract 1 off this index below\n    else:\n        # if not keeping the teleports, keep only the fluorescence on each trial\n        start_inds = trial_starts.tolist()\n        stop_inds = teleports.tolist()\n\n    for i, (start, stop) in enumerate(zip(start_inds, stop_inds)):\n        f_[:, start - 1:stop -\n                 1] = nansmooth(f_[:, start-1:stop-1], [0, 15])\n\n            # minimum filter, taking min val over 20 sec\n            flow[:, start-1:stop-1] = ndimage.minimum_filter1d(\n                flow[:, start-1:stop-1], int(300), axis=-1) # was sp.ndimage.filters.minimum_filter1d\n            flow[:, start-1:stop-1] = ndimage.maximum_filter1d(\n                flow[:, start-1:stop-1], int(300), axis=-1)  # max filter with same window (dilation), was sp.ndimage.filters.maximum_filter1d\n\n    # to get deltaF/F: subtract baseline from initial signal, divide by abs(baseline)\n    # baseline can some times end up as negative due to regression\n    dff = np.zeros(f_.shape) * np.nan\n    if subtract_baseline:\n        dff[:, nanmask] = (f_[:, nanmask] - Note that the threshold\n        defaulted to 0.3 in the source function; the analyses always pass 0.5.\n    :return: is_int (array) with a Boolean for each ROI, where putative interneuron is True.\n\n    ** copied from src/reward_relative/spatial.py::is_putative_interneuron **\n    Change: takes the dF/F and speed arrays as arguments instead of reading them off a TwoPUtils \n    `sess` object, which this conversion does not build.\n    \"\"\"\n    speed_corr = np.zeros((dff.shape[0],))\n    nanmask = ~np.isnan(dff[0, :])\n\n    for c in range(dff.shape[0]):\n        speed_corr[c] = np.corrcoef(\n            dff[c, nanmask], speed[nanmask])[0, 1]\n\n    is_int = speed_corr > r_thresh\n\n    return is_int\n\n# end copy-paste\n# =================================================================================================\n\n\ndef get_subjects(datadir=DATADIR):\n    # find all directories in datadir that start with 'sub-'\n    subjects = [d.split('-')[1] for d in os.listdir(datadir) if d.startswith('sub-')]\n    return subjects\n\ndef get_sessions(subject, datadir=DATADIR):\n    subject_dir = os.path.join(datadir, f\"sub-{subject}\")\n    # parse neural activity data\n        deconvolved_data = nwbfile.processing[\"ophys\"][\"Deconvolved\"]\n        spike_data = []\n        nplanes = len(deconvolved_data.roi_response_series)\n        for plane_data in deconvolved_data.roi_response_series.values():\n            roi_data = plane_data.rois[:]\n            iscell = np.array(roi_data['iscell'].to_list())[:,0] == 1\n            spike_data_curr = plane_data.data[:]\n            assert spike_data_curr.shape[1] == iscell.shape[0], f\"Number of neurons in data ({spike_data_curr.shape[1]}) does not match number of iscell entries ({iscell.shape[0]})\"\n            spike_data.append(spike_data_curr[:,iscell])\n        spike_data = np.concatenate(spike_data, axis=1)\n        info['T'], info['nneurons'] = spike_data.shape\n        max_spike_rate = np.max(spike_data)\n        info['max_spike_rate_range'] = (max_spike_rate.min(), max_spike_rate.max())\n        info['neural_time_bin_size'] = nplanes/plane_data.rate*1000 # ms\n\n        behavior_data = nwbfile.processing['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series       \n        istrial = behavior_data['trial number'].data[:] >= 0\n        # reward_amounts are all the same\n        reward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:]\n        info['unique_reward_amounts'] = np.unique(reward_amounts)\n        info['nrewards'] = len(reward_amounts)\n        # autoreward is all 0s, so not useful for modeling, but check that it is all zeros\n        autoreward = behavior_data['autoreward'].data[:]\n        info['unique_autoreward'] = np.unique(autoreward[istrial])\n        # vr environment\n        vr_environment = behavior_data['environment'].data[:] # one value per time point, does this change by session?\n        info['unique_vr_environment'] = np.unique(vr_environment[istrial])\n        licks = behavior_data['lick'].data[:]\n        info['unique_licks'] = np.unique(licks[istrial])\n        position = behavior_data['position'].data[:]\n        info['position_range'] = (position[istrial].min(), position[istrial].max())\n        reward_zone = behavior_data['reward_zone'].data[:]\n        info['unique_reward_zone'] = np.unique(reward_zone[istrial])\n        scanning = behavior_data['scanning'].data[:]\n        info['unique_scanning'] = np.unique(scanning[istrial])\n        speed = behavior_data['speed'].data[:]\n        info['speed_range'] = (speed[istrial].min(), speed[istrial].max())\n        teleport = behavior_data['teleport'].data[:]\n        info['unique_teleport'] = np.unique(teleport[istrial])\n        \n        # trial number and trial start do not agree with each other\n        # use trial_start and teleport to find starts and ends of trials\n        trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx = get_trial_starts_ends(behavior_data['trial_start'].data[:], teleport)\n        trial_length = trial_end_idx - trial_start_idx\n        info['trial_length_range'] = (trial_length.min(), trial_length.max())\n\n        ntrials = len(trial_start_idx)\n        info['ntrials'] = ntrials\n        info['reward_zone_positions'] = np.full((ntrials, 2), np.nan)\n        for trial in range(ntrials):\n            idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial], trial_end_idx[trial], dtype=int)\n            pos_trial = position[idx]\n            reward_zone_trial = reward_zone[idx] > 0\n            if not np.any(reward_zone_trial):\n                continue\n            info['reward_zone_positions'][trial] = [np.min(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial]), np.max(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial])]\n                    \n    return info\n    \ndef get_bin_centers(bin_edges,data):\n    # replace infinity with the max/min of the data for plotting purposes\n    bin_edges_plot = np.array(bin_edges.copy())\n    if np.isinf(bin_edges_plot[0]):\n        bin_edges_plot[0] = data.min()\n        if bin_edges_plot[0] > bin_edges_plot[1]:\n            bin_edges_plot[0] = 2*bin_edges_plot[1] - bin_edges_plot[2] # extrapolate to get a lower value for the first bin edge\n    if np.isinf(bin_edges_plot[-1]):\n        bin_edges_plot[-1] = data.max()\n        if bin_edges_plot[-1] < bin_edges_plot[-2]:\n            bin_edges_plot[-1] = 2*bin_edges_plot[-2] - bin_edges_plot[-3] # extrapolate to get a higher value for the last bin edge\n    bin_centers = (bin_edges_plot[:-1] + bin_edges_plot[1:]) / 2\n    return bin_centers\n    \ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position_curr, reward_zone_curr):\n    ntimepoints = len(position_curr)\n    reward_zone_range = reward_zone_dict[reward_zone_curr]\n    distance_to_reward_zone = np.zeros(ntimepoints)\n    idx_before_zone = position_curr < reward_zone_range[0]\n    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_before_zone] = position_curr[idx_before_zone] - reward_zone_range[0] # Every step of dff is per-cell --\n        # its only cross-cell operation is a Gaussian with sigma 0 on the cell axis -- so this is\n        # numerically identical to subsetting afterwards, on a third of the memory.\n        fluorescence.append(fluorescence_curr[:,iscell])\n        neuropil.append(neuropil_curr[:,iscell])\n    T = fluorescence[0].shape[0]\n    # ms\n    \n    # trial number for each time point\n    trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx = get_trial_starts_ends(behavior_data['trial_start'].data[:], behavior_data['teleport'].data[:])\n    ntrials = len(trial_start_idx)\n    \n    # in seconds        \n    # find the closest timestamp to reward times, not careful for mismatches right now\n    reward_time_indices = np.searchsorted(timestamps, reward_times)\n    err = timestamps[reward_time_indices] - reward_times\n    assert np.all(np.abs(err) <= time_bin_size/2), f\"Reward times do not align with neural time bins, max error: {np.max(np.abs(err))} s\"\n    isreward = np.zeros(T)\n    isreward[reward_time_indices] = 1\n\n    # autoreward is all 0s, so not useful for modeling, but check that it is all zeros\n\n    # vr environment\n    vr_environment = behavior_data['environment'].data[:][:T] # one value per time point, does this change by session?\n    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['environment'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for environment do not match trial number timestamps\"\n\n    # Unshifted, the last sample of every\n        # lap comes out NaN (80 per session here) and one pre-lap sample gets a value instead;\n        # shifted, dff's windows land exactly on the emitted laps. The one cost is that on\n        # keep_teleports sessions the baseline window opens two samples after the teleport onset\n        # rather than their one.\n        dff_curr, events_curr = switching to encourage stable assignments\n    zone_names = sorted(reward_zone_dict.keys())\n    n_states = len(zone_names)\n    zone_ranges = np.array([reward_zone_dict[z] for z in zone_names])  # (n_states, 2)\n    pstay = .9\n    sigma = 10.0  # std dev for Gaussian emission on zone distance\n\n    # transition log-probabilities\n    log_trans = np.full((n_states, n_states), np.log((1 - pstay) / (n_states - 1)))\n    np.fill_diagonal(log_trans, np.log(pstay))\n\n    results = {}\n\n    for mouse_id, positions in reward_zone_positions.items():\n        n_trials = positions.shape[0]\n        startpoints = positions[:,0] # use the start point as it looks cleaner\n        valid = ~np.isnan(startpoints)\n\n        # emission log-probabilities: Gaussian penalty based on distance to zone range\n        # distance is 0 if inside [lo, hi], otherwise distance to nearest edge\n        # for NaN trials, uniform emission (all zeros) so assignment is driven by transitions\n        log_emission = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n        dist = np.maximum(zone_ranges[None, :, 0] - startpoints[valid, None],\n                          startpoints[valid, None] - zone_ranges[None, :, 1]) # negative if inside the zone, positive if outside\n        dist = np.maximum(dist, 0)\n        log_emission[valid] = -0.5 * (dist / sigma) ** 2\n\n        # viterbi forward pass\n        log_delta = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n        psi = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states), dtype=int)\n        log_delta[0] = log_emission[0]  # uniform prior\n        for t in range(1, n_trials):\n            for k in range(n_states):\n                scores = log_delta[t - 1] + log_trans[:, k]\n                psi[t, k] = np.argmax(scores)\n                log_delta[t, k] = scores[psi[t, k]] + log_emission[t, k]\n\n        # backtrack\n        states = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=int)\n        states[-1] = np.argmax(log_delta[-1])\n        for t in range(n_trials - 2, -1, -1):\n            states[t] = psi[t + 1, states[t + 1]]\n\n        # convert state indices to zone names\n        zone_labels = np.array([zone_names[s] for s in states])\n\n        # distance from each trial's startpoint to its assigned zone range\n        assigned_lo = zone_ranges[states, 0]\n        assigned_hi = zone_ranges[states, 1]\n        zone_dist = np.maximum(np.maximum(assigned_lo - startpoints, startpoints - assigned_hi), 0)\n        zone_dist[~valid] = np.nan\n\n        results[mouse_id] = {'zone_labels': zone_labels, 'states': states, 'zone_dist': zone_dist}\n\n    return results\n 207→def discretize_distance(d):\n   208→    \"\"\"7 classes, exactly as specified by the decoder task.\"\"\"\n   209→    out = np.full(d.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)     # d == 0 -> in the reward zone\n np.asarray(seg['planeIdx'].data[:]).astype(int)\n   245→\n   246→        # Concatenate the per-plane RoiResponseSeries; their ROI ids index the shared\n   247→        # PlaneSegmentation table in 250→        for pn in plane_names:\n   251→            rrs = oph['Fluorescence'].roi_response_series[pn]\n   252→            rid = np.asarray(rrs.rois.data[:], dtype=int)\n   253→            sel = iscell[rid]                       # only load curated cells\n   254→            roi_ids.append(rid[sel])\n   255→            f_list.append(np.asarray(rrs.data[:, :], dtype=np.float32)[:, sel].T)\n   256→            nrs = oph['Neuropil'].roi_response_series[pn]\n   257→            fneu_list.append(np.asarray(nrs.data[:, :], dtype=np.float32)[:, sel].T)\n   258→        f = np.concatenate(f_list, axis=0)\n   259→        f_neu = np.concatenate(fneu_list, axis=0)\n   260→        roi_ids = np.concatenate(roi_ids)\n   261→        imaging_rate = float(nwb.imaging_planes['ImagingPlane'].imaging_rate)\n   262→        location = nwb.imaging_planes['ImagingPlane'].location\n   263→\n   264→    # A handful of the 2-plane files (10 of 152) carry one more imaging frame than the\n   265→    # 2P-aligned VR data. The VR alignment defines the common sample grid, so trim the\n   266→    # trailing frames of every stream to the shorter length.\n   267→    n = min(len(ts), f.shape[1])\n   268→    n_trimmed = max(len(ts), f.shape[1]) - n\n   269→    ts = ts[:n]\n   270→    beh = {k: v[:n] for k, v in beh.items()}\n   271→    f, f_neu = f[:, :n], f_neu[:, :n]\n   272→\n   273→    return dict(path=path, scene=scene, subject=subject, session_id=session_id,\n   274→                ts=ts, reward_times=reward_times, f=f, f_neu=f_neu,\n   275→                roi_ids=roi_ids, plane_of_cell=plane_idx[roi_ids], n_trimmed=n_trimmed,\n   276→                imaging_rate=imaging_rate, location=location, **beh)\n   277→\n   278→\n   279→def convert_session(path, show_processing=False, plot_dir='/app', signal='dff'):\n   280→    \"\"\"Convert one NWB session into per-trial neural / input / output arrays.\"\"\"\n   281→    t0 = time.time()\n   282→    raw = read_session(path)\n   283→    t_read = time.time() - t0\n   284→\n   285→    ts = raw['ts']\n   286→    pos = raw['position']\n   287→    speed = raw['speed']\n   288→    lick = raw['lick']\n   289→    rzone_flag = raw['reward_zone']\n   290→    morph = raw['environment']\n   291→    scanning = raw['scanning']\n   292→\n   293→    si = np.where(raw['trial_start'] == 1)[0]\n   294→    ti = np.where(raw['teleport'] == 1)[0]\n   295→    assert len(si) == len(ti), f'{path}: {len(si)} starts vs {len(ti)} teleports'\n   296→    # guard against a trial starting on frame 0 (window would wrap around)\n   297→    keep = (si >= 1) & (ti <= len(ts))\n   298→    n_trials_out_of_range = int((~keep).sum())\n   299→    si, ti = si[keep], ti[keep]\n   300→    assert np.all(ti > si), f'{path}: teleport before trial start'\n   301→    ntrials_all = len(si)\n   302→\n   303→    # The frame period is the sampling interval of the (already 2P-aligned) VR data.\n   304→    dt = float(np.median(np.diff(ts)))\n   305→    frame_rate = 1.0 / dt\n   306→\n   307→    # ---- neural: dF/F + deconvolved events (reference preprocessing.dff) -------------\n   308→    t1 = time.time()\n   309→    dff, events = dff_and_events(raw['f'], raw['f_neu'], si, ti, frame_rate)\n   310→    t_dff = time.time() - t1\n   311→\n   312→    valid = ~np.isnan(dff[0, :])          # frames inside a trial window\n   313→\n   314→    # ---- neuron curation: putative interneurons (r(dF/F, speed) > 0.5) ---------------\n   315→    sp_v = speed[valid].astype(np.float32)\n   316→    d_v = dff[:, valid]\n   317→    sp_c = sp_v - sp_v.mean()\n   318→    d_c = d_v - d_v.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n   319→    denom = np.sqrt((d_c ** 2).sum(axis=1) * (sp_c ** 2).sum())\n   320→    with np.errstate(invalid='ignore', divide='ignore'):\n   321→        r_speed = (d_c @ sp_c) / denom\n   322→    r_speed = np.nan_to_num(r_speed, nan=0.0)\n   323→    keep_cells = r_speed <= INTERNEURON_SPEED_CORR_THR\n   324→    n_interneuron = int((~keep_cells).sum())\n   325→    dff = dff[keep_cells]\n   326→    events = events[keep_cells]\n   327→    plane_of_cell = raw['plane_of_cell'][keep_cells]\n   328→\n   329→    # ---- per-trial task variables (reference behavior.get_trial_types/_reward_zones) --\n   330→    reward_idx = np.searchsorted(ts, raw['reward_times'])\n   331→    zone_lab = zone_labels_from_scene(raw['scene'], ntrials_all)\n   332→    zone_start = np.array([REWARD_ZONE_DICT[z][0] for z in zone_lab])\n   333→    zone_end = np.array([REWARD_ZONE_DICT[z][1] for z in zone_lab])\n   334→    zone_code = np.array([ZONE_ORDER.index(z) for z in zone_lab], dtype=np.int64)\n   335→\n   336→    isreward = np.zeros(ntrials_all, dtype=np.int64)\n   337→    trial_morph = np.zeros(ntrials_all, dtype=np.int64)\n   338→    lick_error = np.zeros(ntrials_all, dtype=bool)\n   339→    scan_bad = np.zeros(ntrials_all, dtype=bool)\n   340→    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(si, ti)):\n   341→        has_reward = np.any((reward_idx >= s) & (reward_idx < e))\n   342→        isreward[i] = int(has_reward and np.any(rzone_flag[s:e] > 0))\n   343→        mvals = np.unique(morph[s:e])\n   344→        trial_morph[i] = int(mvals[0]) if len(mvals) == 1 else int(np.round(np.median(morph[s:e])))\n   345→        L = lick[s:e]\n   346→        lick_error[i] = (np.sum(L > LICK_ERROR_COUNT_THR) / len(L)) > LICK_ERROR_FRAC_THR\n   347→        scan_bad[i] = np.any(scanning[s - 1:e - 1] != 1)\n   348→\n   349→    # previous-trial outcome; index 0 is undefined -> 0 (see CONVERSION_NOTES Step 5)\n   350→    prev_reward = np.zeros(ntrials_all, dtype=np.int64)\n   351→    prev_reward[1:] = isreward[:-1]\n   352→\n   353→    # ---- assemble per-trial arrays ---------------------------------------------------\n   354→    neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials = [], [], []\n   355→    kept_trial_idx = []\n   356→    n_dropped_lick, n_dropped_scan, n_dropped_nan = 0, 0, 0\n   357→    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(si, ti)):\n   358→        if lick_error[i]:\n   359→            n_dropped_lick += 1\n   360→            continue\n   361→        if scan_bad[i]:\n   362→            n_dropped_scan += 1\n   363→            continue\n   364→        sl = slice(s - 1, e - 1)\n   365→        act = (events if signal == 'events' else dff)[:, sl]\n   366→        if act.shape[1] < 2 or not np.all(np.isfinite(act)):\n   367→            n_dropped_nan += 1\n   368→            continue\n   369→\n   370→        T = act.shape[1]\n   371→        p = pos[sl]\n   372→        sp_t = speed[sl]\n   373→        lk = lick[sl]\n   374→\n   375→        inp = np.empty((4, T), dtype=np.float32)\n   376→        inp[0] = ts[sl] - ts[s]                 # t = 0 at the trial_start frame\n   377→        inp[1] = trial_morph[i]\n   378→        inp[2] = i\n   379→        inp[3] = prev_reward[i]\n   380→\n   381→        out = np.empty((6, T), dtype=np.int64)\n   382→        out[0] = discretize_distance(signed_distance_to_zone(p, zone_start[i], zone_end[i]))\n   383→        out[1] = np.digitize(p, POS_EDGES)\n   384→        out[2] = np.digitize(sp_t, SPEED_EDGES)\n   385→        out[3] = (lk > 0).astype(np.int64)\n   386→        out[4] = zone_code[i]\n   387→        out[5] = isreward[i]\n   388→\n   389→        neural_trials.append(np.ascontiguousarray(act, dtype=np.float32))\n   390→        input_trials.append(inp)\n   391→        output_trials.append(out)\n   392→        kept_trial_idx.append(i)\n   393→\n   394→    info = dict(\n   395→        path=path, subject=raw['subject'], session_id=raw['session_id'],\n   396→        scene=raw['scene'], location=raw['location'],\n   397→        n_trials_all=ntrials_all, n_trials_kept=len(neural_trials),\n   398→        n_dropped_lick=n_dropped_lick, n_dropped_scan=n_dropped_scan,\n   399→        n_dropped_nan=n_dropped_nan,\n   400→        n_cells_iscell=int(raw['f'].shape[0]), n_interneuron=n_interneuron,\n   401→        n_frames_trimmed=int(raw['n_trimmed']),\n   402→        n_trials_out_of_range=n_trials_out_of_range,\n   403→        n_cells=int(dff.shape[0]), dt=dt, imaging_rate=raw['imaging_rate'],\n   404→        n_omission=int((isreward == 0).sum()),\n   405→        zone_labels=''.join(zone_lab), morph=trial_morph.tolist(),\n   406→        t_read=t_read, t_dff=t_dff, t_total=time.time() - t0,\n   407→        dff_median=float(np.nanmedian(dff[:, valid])),\n   408→        dff_p99=float(np.nanpercentile(dff[:, valid], 99)),\n   409→        events_frac_zero=float(np.mean(events[:, valid] == 0)),\n   410→    )\n   411→\n   412→    if show_processing:\n   413→        plot_processing(raw, dff, events, si, ti, valid, zone_start, zone_end,\n   414→                        zone_lab, isreward, kept_trial_idx, neural_trials,\n   415→                        input_trials, output_trials, r_speed, keep_cells,\n   416→                        plot_dir, signal)\n   417→\n   418→    brain_region_idx = np.zeros(dff.shape[0], dtype=np.int64)\n   419→    return dict(neural=neural_trials, input=input_trials, output=output_trials,\n   420→                brain_region_idx=brain_region_idx, info=info,\n   421→                plane_of_cell=plane_of_cell)\n   422→\n   423→\n   424→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   425→# Diagnostic plots\n   426→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   427→\n   428→def plot_processing(raw, dff, events, si, ti, valid, zone_start, zone_end, zone_lab,\n   429→                    isreward, kept_trial_idx, neural_trials, input_trials,\n   430→                    output_trials, r_speed, keep_cells, plot_dir, signal='dff'):\n   431→    import matplotlib\n   432→    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   433→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   434→\n   435→    tag = f\"{raw['subject']}_ses-{raw['session_id']}\"\n   436→    ts, pos, speed, lick = raw['ts'], raw['position'], raw['speed'], raw['lick']\n   437→\n   438→    fig, axes = plt.subplots(9, 1, figsize=(20, 26))\n   439→\n   440→    # --- 1. raw F / neuropil / baseline-corrected dF/F for one example cell ----------\n   441→    cell = int(np.nanargmax(np.nanstd(dff, axis=1)))\n   442→    w = slice(si[0] - 1, ti[4] - 1)          # first five trials\n   443→    ax = axes[0]\n   444→    ax.plot(ts[w], raw['f'][cell, w], lw=0.7, label='raw F')\n   445→    ax.plot(ts[w], raw['f_neu'][cell, w], lw=0.7, label='neuropil')\n   446→    for s, e in zip(si[:5], ti[:5]):\n   447→        ax.axvspan(ts[s], ts[e - 1], color='0.9', zorder=0)\n   448→    ax.set_title(f'{tag}: raw fluorescence, example cell {cell} (grey = trials)')\n   449→    ax.legend(loc='upper right')\n   450→    ax.set_ylabel('a.u.')\n   451→\n   452→    ax = axes[1]\n   453→    ax.plot(ts[w], dff[cell, w], lw=0.8, label='dF/F (smoothed)')\n   454→    ax.plot(ts[w], events[cell, w], lw=0.8, label='events (OASIS)')\n   455→    for s, e in zip(si[:5], ti[:5]):\n   456→        ax.axvspan(ts[s], ts[e - 1], color='0.9', zorder=0)\n   457→    ax.set_title('dF/F and deconvolved events (NaN between trials = gaps)')\n   458→    ax.legend(loc='upper right')\n   459→\n   460→    # --- 2. dF/F distribution and interneuron filter ---------------------------------\n   461→    ax = axes[2]\n   462→    ax.hist(dff[:, valid].ravel()[::101], bins=200, log=True)\n   463→    ax.set_title(f'dF/F distribution (in-trial samples), median='\n   464→                 f'{np.nanmedian(dff[:, valid]):.3f}')\n   465→    ax.set_xlabel('dF/F')\n   466→\n   467→    ax = axes[3]\n   468→    ax.hist(r_speed, bins=100)\n   469→    ax.axvline(INTERNEURON_SPEED_CORR_THR, color='r')\n   470→    ax.set_title(f'Pearson r(dF/F, speed) per cell; {int((~keep_cells).sum())} of '\n   471→                 f'{len(keep_cells)} excluded as putative interneurons (r > 0.5)')\n   472→\n   473→    # --- 3. behaviour with trial boundaries and reward zone --------------------------\n   474→    w2 = slice(si[0] - 1, ti[7] - 1)\n   475→    ax = axes[4]\n   476→    ax.plot(ts[w2], pos[w2], lw=1, color='k', label='position')\n   477→    for j, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(si[:8], ti[:8])):\n   478→        ax.axvline(ts[s], color='g', lw=0.8)\n   479→        ax.axvline(ts[e - 1], color='r', lw=0.8)\n   480→        ax.fill_between([ts[s], ts[e - 1]], zone_start[j], zone_end[j],\n   481→                        color='orange', alpha=.3)\n   482→    ax.set_ylabel('position (cm)')\n   483→    ax.set_title('position, trial starts (green) / ends (red), reward zone (orange)')\n   484→\n   485→    ax = axes[5]\n   486→    ax.plot(ts[w2], speed[w2], lw=1, label='speed (cm/s)')\n   487→    ax.plot(ts[w2], lick[w2] * 5, lw=1, label='lick count x5')\n   488→    rt = raw['reward_times']\n   489→    rt = rt[(rt >= ts[w2.start]) & (rt <= ts[w2.stop])]\n   490→    ax.plot(rt, np.zeros_like(rt) + 50, 'v', color='m', label='reward')\n   491→    ax.legend(loc='upper right')\n   492→    ax.set_title('speed / licks / reward delivery')\n   493→\n   494→    # --- 4. discretisation check: continuous vs class, one trial ---------------------\n   495→    tr = min(5, len(neural_trials) - 1)\n   496→    orig = kept_trial_idx[tr]\n   497→    sl = slice(si[orig] - 1, ti[orig] - 1)\n   498→    tt = input_trials[tr][0]\n   499→    d = signed_distance_to_zone(pos[sl], zone_start[orig], zone_end[orig])\n   500→    ax = axes[6]\n   501→    ax.plot(tt, pos[sl], 'k', label='position (cm)')\n   502→    ax.plot(tt, d, 'b', label='distance to reward zone (cm)')\n   503→    for y in (-50, -10, 0, 10, 50):\n   504→        ax.axhline(y, color='b', ls=':', lw=.5)\n   505→    ax2 = ax.twinx()\n   506→    ax2.step(tt, output_trials[tr][0], 'r', where='post', label='distance class')\n   507→    ax2.step(tt, output_trials[tr][1], 'g', where='post', label='position class')\n   508→    ax2.set_ylabel('class')\n   509→    ax.legend(loc='upper left')\n   510→    ax2.legend(loc='lower right')\n   511→    ax.set_title(f'trial {orig}: discretisation of distance-to-zone and position '\n   512→                 f'(zone {zone_lab[orig]}, rewarded={isreward[orig]})')\n   513→    ax.set_xlabel('time from trial start (s)')\n   514→\n   515→    ax = axes[7]\n   516→    ax.plot(tt, speed[sl], 'k', label='speed (cm/s)')\n   517→    for y in (2, 10, 20, 40):\n   518→        ax.axhline(y, color='b', ls=':', lw=.5)\n   519→    ax2 = ax.twinx()\n   520→    ax2.step(tt, output_trials[tr][2], 'r', where='post', label='speed class')\n   521→    ax2.step(tt, output_trials[tr][3], 'g', where='post', label='lick class')\n   522→    ax.plot(tt, lick[sl], 'm', lw=.8, label='lick count')\n   523→    ax.legend(loc='upper left')\n   524→    ax2.legend(loc='lower right')\n   525→    ax.set_title('discretisation of speed and licking')\n   526→    ax.set_xlabel('time from trial start (s)')\n   527→\n   528→    # --- 5. final aligned trial: neural raster + outputs ------------------------------\n   529→    ax = axes[8]\n   530→    act = neural_trials[tr]\n   531→    order = np.argsort(np.argmax(act, axis=1))\n   532→    ax.imshow(act[order][:200], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest',\n   533→              extent=[tt[0], tt[-1], 200, 0], cmap='magma',\n   534→              vmax=np.percentile(act, 99.5))\n   535→    ax2 = ax.twinx()\n   536→    ax2.plot(tt, pos[sl], 'c', lw=1.5)\n   537→    ax2.set_ylabel('position (cm)', color='c')\n   538→    ax.set_xlabel('time from trial start (s)')\n   539→    ax.set_ylabel('neuron (sorted)')\n   540→    ax.set_title(f'converted trial: neural data as stored ({signal}, first 200 cells) with '\n   541→                 'position overlaid -- checks temporal alignment')\n   542→\n   543→    fig.tight_layout()\n   544→    out = os.path.join(plot_dir, f'processing_{tag}.png')\n   545→    fig.savefig(out, dpi=90)\n   546→    plt.close(fig)\n   547→    print(f'  wrote {out}', flush=True)\n   548→\n   549→\n   550→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   551→# Driver\n   552→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   553→\n   554→def _worker(args):\n   555→    path, show, plot_dir, signal = args\n   556→    warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n   557→    try:\n   558→        return convert_session(path, show_processing=show, plot_dir=plot_dir,\n   559→                               signal=signal)\n   560→    except Exception as exc:          # keep the run alive, report at the end\n   561→        import traceback\n   562→        return dict(error=f'{path}: {exc}\\n{traceback.format_exc()}')\n   563→\n   564→\n   565→def main():\n   566→    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n   567→    ap.add_argument('outfile')\n   568→    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True)\n   569→    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true')\n   570→    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true')\n   571→    ap.add_argument('--nproc', type=int, default=16)\n   572→    # dF/F is the default neural signal: it is the paper's own `preprocessing.dff`\n   573→    # output, and it decodes markedly better than the OASIS-deconvolved \"events\" for a\n   574→    # per-timepoint linear decoder (see CONVERSION_NOTES Step 7).\n   575→    ap.add_argument('--signal', choices=['dff', 'events'], default='dff')\n   576→    args = ap.parse_args()\n   577→\n   578→    files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(DATA_ROOT, 'sub-*', '*.nwb')))\n   579→    if args.sample:\n   580→        # two sessions from different mice, one single-plane and one 2-plane\n   581→        files = [f for f in files if 'sub-m11_ses-03' in f or 'sub-m17_ses-08' in f]\n   582→    print(f'Converting {len(files)} sessions with {args.nproc} workers', flush=True)\n   583→\n   584→    show_n = 2 if args.show_processing else 0\n   585→    tasks = [(f, i < show_n, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(args.outfile)) or '.',\n   586→              args.signal) for i, f in enumerate(files)]\n   587→\n   588→    t0 = time.time()\n   589→    results = []\n   590→    ctx = mp.get_context('spawn')\n   591→    with ctx.Pool(min(args.nproc, len(files))) as pool:\n   592→        for k, res in enumerate(pool.imap(_worker, tasks)):\n   593→            if 'error' in res:\n   594→                print('ERROR', res['error'], flush=True)\n   595→                raise RuntimeError(res['error'])\n   596→            i = res['info']\n   597→            el = time.time() - t0\n   598→            print(f\"[{k+1}/{len(files)}] {i['subject']} ses-{i['session_id']} \"\n   599→                  f\"{i['scene']}: {i['n_cells']} cells \"\n   600→                  f\"({i['n_cells_iscell']} iscell, {i['n_interneuron']} interneurons), \"\n   601→                  f\"{i['n_trials_kept']}/{i['n_trials_all']} trials \"\n   602→                  f\"(dropped lick={i['n_dropped_lick']} scan={i['n_dropped_scan']} \"\n   603→                  f\"nan={i['n_dropped_nan']}) | read {i['t_read']:.1f}s \"\n   604→                  f\"dff {i['t_dff']:.1f}s total {i['t_total']:.1f}s | \"\n   605→                  f\"elapsed {el/60:.1f} min\", flush=True)\n   606→            results.append(res)\n   607→\n   608→    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ assemble\n   609→    subjects = sorted({r['info']['subject'] for r in results},\n   610→                      key=lambda s: int(s[1:]))\n   611→    data = {\n   612→        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n   613→        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n   614→        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n   615→        'subjects': subjects,\n   616→        'subject_idx': np.array([subjects.index(r['info']['subject']) for r in results],\n   617→                                dtype=np.int64),\n   618→        'brain_regions': ['CA1'],\n   619→        'brain_region_idx': [r['brain_region_idx'] for r in results],\n   620→        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n   621→        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   622→        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n   623→    }\n   624→\n   625→    dts = np.array([r['info']['dt'] for r in results])\n   626→    ntr = np.array([r['info']['n_trials_kept'] for r in results])\n   627→    ncell = np.array([r['info']['n_cells'] for r in results])\n   628→    trial_durs = np.concatenate([[inp[0, -1] for inp in r['input']] for r in results])\n   629→\n   630→    data['metadata'] = {\n   631→        'task_description': (\n   632→            'Head-fixed mice run laps on a 450 cm virtual linear track for a hidden 50 cm '\n   633→            'reward zone at one of three locations (A 80-130 cm, B 200-250 cm, C 320-370 cm) '\n   634→            'in one of two visual environments (ENV1/ENV2). The reward zone is moved to a new '\n   635→            'location after 30 trials on \"switch\" days, and reward is randomly omitted on ~15% '\n   636→            'of trials. Outputs to decode are the signed distance to the reward zone, absolute '\n   637→            'track position, running speed, licking, which reward zone is active, and whether '\n   638→            'the trial was rewarded. python3\n\"\"\"\nConvert the Sosa, Plitt & Giocomo (2025) hippocampal CA1 2-photon dataset\n(DANDI 001361, NWB format) into the decoder-training dictionary format.\n\nUsage:\n    python -u convert_data.py <outpicklefile> [--full | --sample] [--show-processing]\n\nProcessing follows the authors' reference code (/app/code, `reward_relative` package):\n  * dF/F is recomputed from the raw `Fluorescence` / `Neuropil` traces with\n    `preprocessing.dff`'s exact algorithm (neuropil subtraction with coefficient 0.7,\n    per-trial `maximin` baseline over a 20 s window, 2-sample Gaussian smoothing).\n    The OASIS-deconvolved \"events\" of the same function are also computed and can be\n    stored instead with `--signal events`; dF/F is the default (see CONVERSION_NOTES).\n  * Trials are delimited by the `[trial_start - 1, teleport - 1)` frame window used\n    throughout the reference code (`preprocessing.dff`, `glmUtils.get_timeseries_data`).\n  * Per-trial task variables (reward zone identity, environment, reward outcome) are\n    derived exactly as in `behavior.get_reward_zones` / `behavior.get_trial_types`.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport glob\nimport os\nimport pickle\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport warnings\nimport multiprocessing as mp\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport scipy as sp\nfrom scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, gaussian_filter1d, maximum_filter1d, minimum_filter1d\n\nfrom pynwb import NWBHDF5IO\n\nDATA_ROOT = '/app/data'\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Constants taken from the reference code / methods\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# reward_relative.behavior.reward_zone_dict: 'X'->A, 'Y'->B, 'Z'->C (start, end in cm)\nREWARD_ZONE_DICT = {'A': (80.0, 130.0), 'B': (200.0, 250.0), 'C': (320.0, 370.0)}\nZONE_ORDER = ['A', 'B', 'C']\nCHANGE_TRIAL = 30            # \"Each switch occurred after 30
